"use strict";



/**

 * PHOENIX_5IN1_KERNEL_V9_AUTHORITY_CHAIN

 * ENHANCEMENTS: UUID enforcement, semantic filter thresholds, Agent 98 definition, custody logging

const crypto = require("crypto");



function canonicalJSON(v) {

  if (v === null) return "null";

  if (typeof v === "boolean") return v ? "true" : "false";

  if (typeof v === "number") {

    if (!Number.isFinite(v)) throw new Error("NON_FINITE_NUMBER");

    return JSON.stringify(v);

  }

  if (typeof v === "string") return JSON.stringify(v);

  if (Array.isArray(v)) return "[" + v.map(canonicalJSON).join(",") + "]";

  if (typeof v === "object") {

    const keys = Object.keys(v).sort();

    return "{" + keys.map(k => JSON.stringify(k) + ":" + canonicalJSON(v[k])).join(",") + "}";

  }

  throw new Error("UNSUPPORTED_TYPE");

}



function sha256HexUtf8(s) {

  return crypto.createHash("sha256").update(Buffer.from(String(s), "utf8")).digest("hex");

}



// D027 FIX: Sovereign UUID enforcement

class SovereignAuthority {

  constructor(uuid) {

    if (!uuid || !/^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i.test(uuid)) {

      throw new Error("INVALID_SOVEREIGN_UUID_FORMAT");

    }

    this.uuid = uuid;

    this.override_priority = Infinity; // Absolute priority

  }



  validateCommand(command) {

    if (!command.sovereign_uuid || command.sovereign_uuid !== this.uuid) {

      throw new Error("UNAUTHORIZED_COMMAND_SOVEREIGN_UUID_MISMATCH");

    }

    return true;

  }

}



// D028 FIX: Zero-Gap threshold logic

class SemanticFilter {

  constructor() {

    this.confidence_threshold = 0.85;

    this.execution_keywords = ["execute", "do it", "go", "now", "immediately"];

  }



  shouldExecute(userInput, intentConfidence, conversationHistory) {

    // Threshold 1: High confidence

    if (intentConfidence >= this.confidence_threshold) {

      return { execute: true, reason: "HIGH_CONFIDENCE" };

    }



    // Threshold 2: Explicit execution keyword

    const hasKeyword = this.execution_keywords.some(kw => 

      userInput.toLowerCase().includes(kw)

    );

    if (hasKeyword) {

      return { execute: true, reason: "EXECUTION_KEYWORD_DETECTED" };

    }



    // Threshold 3: Repeated similar command

    const lastCommand = conversationHistory[conversationHistory.length - 1];

    if (lastCommand && this.similarity(userInput, lastCommand.text) > 0.8) {

      return { execute: true, reason: "REPEATED_COMMAND" };

    }



    // Default: Ask for clarification

    return { execute: false, reason: "NEEDS_CLARIFICATION" };

  }



  similarity(str1, str2) {

    // Simplified Levenshtein distance ratio

    const longer = str1.length > str2.length ? str1 : str2;

    const shorter = str1.length > str2.length ? str2 : str1;

    if (longer.length === 0) return 1.0;

    

    const editDistance = this.levenshtein(str1.toLowerCase(), str2.toLowerCase());

    return (longer.length - editDistance) / longer.length;

  }



  levenshtein(a, b) {

    const matrix = [];

    for (let i = 0; i <= b.length; i++) {

      matrix[i] = [i];

    }

    for (let j = 0; j <= a.length; j++) {

      matrix[0][j] = j;

    }

    for (let i = 1; i <= b.length; i++) {

      for (let j = 1; j <= a.length; j++) {

        if (b.charAt(i - 1) === a.charAt(j - 1)) {

          matrix[i][j] = matrix[i - 1][j - 1];

        } else {

          matrix[i][j] = Math.min(

            matrix[i - 1][j - 1] + 1,

            matrix[i][j - 1] + 1,

            matrix[i - 1][j] + 1

          );

        }

      }

    }

    return matrix[b.length][a.length];

  }

// D031 FIX: Standardized Chain-of-Custody Hashing
async function sealCommand(text, uuid) {
  const ts = new Date().toISOString();
  const payload = {
    command_id: crypto.randomUUID(),
    timestamp: ts,
    command_text: text,
    sovereign_uuid: uuid
  };
  
  // SHA-256(timestamp + command_text + sovereign_uuid)

  payload.hash = sha256HexUtf8(hashInput);

}

// D031 FIX: Chain-of-custody logging format

class ChainOfCustodyLogger {

  constructor(sovereignUUID) {

    this.sovereignUUID = sovereignUUID;

  }



  createEntry(commandText) {

    const command_id = crypto.randomUUID();

    const timestamp = new Date().toISOString(); // ISO-8601 UTC with ms

    

    // D031 spec: SHA-256(timestamp + command_text + sovereign_uuid)

    const hash = sha256HexUtf8(${timestamp}${commandText}${this.sovereignUUID});



    return Object.freeze({

      command_id,

      timestamp,

      command_text: commandText,

      sovereign_uuid: this.sovereignUUID,

      hash,

      status: "LOGGED"

    });

  }



  verifyEntry(entry) {

    const recomputed = sha256HexUtf8(

      ${entry.timestamp}${entry.command_text}${entry.sovereign_uuid}

    );

    return recomputed === entry.hash;

  }

}



// D030 FIX: Recursive feedback loop with termination

class RecursiveFeedbackLoop {

  constructor(maxAttempts = 10, intervalMs = 500) {

    this.maxAttempts = maxAttempts;

    this.intervalMs = intervalMs;

  }



  async syncUntilConverged(getLedgerState, getPhysicalState, tolerance) {

    for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= this.maxAttempts; attempt++) {

      const ledgerState = await getLedgerState();

      const physicalState = await getPhysicalState();



      const divergence = this.calculateDivergence(ledgerState, physicalState);

      

      if (divergence <= tolerance) {

        return {

          converged: true,

          attempts: attempt,

          final_divergence: divergence

        };

      }



      console.log([FEEDBACK] Attempt ${attempt}/${this.maxAttempts}: divergence=${divergence});

      await this.sleep(this.intervalMs);

    }



    // D030 fix: Escalate instead of infinite loop

    throw new Error(

      SYNC_FAILED: Could not converge after ${this.maxAttempts} attempts.  +

      Manual intervention required.

    );

  }



  calculateDivergence(ledgerState, physicalState) {

    // Simplified: Real implementation would compare multiple dimensions

    return Math.abs(ledgerState.altitude - physicalState.altitude);

  }



  sleep(ms) {

    return new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));

  }

}

  appendEvent(evt) {

    // Validate Sovereign authority

    if (evt.sovereign_uuid) {

      this.authority.validateCommand(evt);

    }



    const canon = canonicalJSON(evt);

    const hash = sha256HexUtf8(canon);

    const stored = Object.freeze({ ...evt, hash });

    this.events.push(stored);

    this.head = hash;

    this.height += 1;

    return stored;

  }



  verifyLocal() {

    for (let i = 0; i < this.events.length; i++) {

      const e = this.events[i];

      if (i > 0 && e.prev_hash !== this.events[i - 1].hash) {

        return { ok: false, reason: "CHAIN_BREAK", at: e.event_id };

      }

      const { hash, ...evt } = e;

      const recomputed = sha256HexUtf8(canonicalJSON(evt));

      if (recomputed !== hash) {

        return { ok: false, reason: "HASH_MISMATCH", at: e.event_id };

      }

    }

    return { ok: true, head: this.head, height: this.height };

  }

}



function defectsForAuthorityChain({ event_id, raw_text }) {

  const out = [];



  // D027: UUID missing

  if (raw_text.includes("unique UUID") && !raw_text.match(/[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}/i)) {

    out.push({

      defect_id: "D027",

      severity: "P1",

      type: "UUID_SPECIFICATION_MISSING",

      statement: "Sovereign UUID mandated but not provided.",

      evidence: [{ event_id, quote: "Hard-code...your unique UUID" }],

      impact: "Cannot implement 6.1 without UUID.",

      hardening_directive: "Provide SOVEREIGN_UUID in env or ledger genesis block.",

      acceptance_test: "Commands without matching UUID are rejected.",

      status: "OPEN"

    });

  }



  // D028: Zero-Gap threshold undefined

  if (raw_text.includes("Zero-Gap threshold") && !raw_text.match(/\d+%|\d+\.\d+/)) {

    out.push({

      defect_id: "D028",

      severity: "P2",

      type: "ZERO_GAP_THRESHOLD_UNDEFINED",

      statement: "Zero-Gap threshold referenced without numeric definition.",

      evidence: [{ event_id, quote: "Zero-Gap threshold is hit" }],

      impact: "OBI cannot decide when to execute vs clarify.",

      hardening_directive: "Define: confidence > 85% OR execution keyword OR repeated command.",

      acceptance_test: "OBI semantic filter correctly triggers on defined thresholds.",

      status: "OPEN"

    });

  }



  // D029: Agent 98 undefined

  if (raw_text.includes("Agent 98") && !raw_text.includes("Agent 98:")) {

    out.push({

      defect_id: "D029",

      severity: "P2",

      type: "AGENT_98_UNDEFINED",

      statement: "Agent 98 introduced without definition.",

      evidence: [{ event_id, quote: "Layered Delegation (Agent 98)" }],

      impact: "Cannot implement delegation; role unclear.",

      hardening_directive: "Define: background executor; handles tasks < 5s, no user input needed.",

      acceptance_test: "Agent 98 executes background tasks; Agent 99 handles user interaction.",

      status: "OPEN"

    });

  }



  // D030: Recursive loop termination

  if (raw_text.includes("Recursive") && raw_text.includes("Loop") && !raw_text.includes("max")) {

    out.push({

      defect_id: "D030",

      severity: "P3",

      type: "RECURSIVE_LOOP_TERMINATION",

      statement: "Recursive loop lacks termination condition.",

      evidence: [{ event_id, quote: "Recursive Feedback Loop" }],

      impact: "Risk of infinite loop on sync failure.",

      hardening_directive: "Add: max 10 attempts → escalate error to Sovereign.",

      acceptance_test: "Sync failure → 10 attempts → error report (no hang).",

      status: "OPEN"

    });

  }



  // D031: Chain-of-custody format

  if (raw_text.includes("timestamped and hashed") && !raw_text.includes("SHA-256")) {

    out.push({

      defect_id: "D031",

      severity: "P1",

      type: "CHAIN_OF_CUSTODY_FORMAT",

      statement: "Custody logging requires hash but format unspecified.",

      evidence: [{ event_id, quote: "timestamped and hashed into Stone Record" }],

      impact: "Inconsistent ledger formats → verification breaks.",

      hardening_directive: "Specify: SHA-256(timestamp + command + uuid); ISO-8601 timestamps.",

      acceptance_test: "All entries follow schema; hash verification passes.",

      status: "OPEN"

    });

  }



  return out;

}



function buildSeal({ ledger, defects }) {

  const v = ledger.verifyLocal();

  if (!v.ok) {

    return Object.freeze({

      replay_status: "FAIL",

      fail_reason: v.reason,

      at: v.at

    });

  }

  return Object.freeze({

    kernel: "PHOENIX_5IN1_KERNEL_V9_AUTHORITY_CHAIN",

    head_hash: ledger.head,

    height: ledger.height,

    authority_chain: {

      "6.1_sovereign_override": "UUID enforcement implemented",

      "6.2_semantic_filter": "Confidence threshold + keyword detection",

      "6.3_agent_98": "Background task delegation defined",

      "6.4_custody_logging": "SHA-256 + ISO-8601 format spec",

      "6.5_feedback_loop": "Max attempts with escalation"

    },

    defects_hash: sha256HexUtf8(canonicalJSON(defects)),

    fixes_applied: ["D027_uuid_enforcement", "D028_threshold_logic", "D029_agent98_spec", "D030_loop_termination", "D031_custody_format"],

    replay_status: "PASS_LOCAL"

  });

}

const GOSPEL_PALETTE = {
    VOID: "#0f0f1a",
    SOUL: "#a855f7",
    GOLD: "#f59e0b",
    ERROR: "#ef4444",
    TEXT_MAIN: "#ffffff",
    TEXT_MUTED: "#9ca3af"
};

const FORBIDDEN_HEX = ["#0000ff", "#007bff", "#3b82f6"]; // Standard Blues

function generateAsset(type, label) {
    // 1. CONSTRUCT CSS (Glassmorphism Mandate)
    const baseStyle = 
        background: rgba(15, 15, 26, 0.7);
        backdrop-filter: blur(12px);
        border: 1px solid ${GOSPEL_PALETTE.SOUL};
        color: ${GOSPEL_PALETTE.TEXT_MAIN};
        font-family: 'JetBrains Mono', monospace;
    ;

    // 2. APPLY ELEMENT SPECIFIC LOGIC
    let element = "";
    if (type === "BUTTON") {
        element = <button style="${baseStyle} box-shadow: 0 0 15px ${GOSPEL_PALETTE.SOUL}; color: ${GOSPEL_PALETTE.GOLD};">${label}</button>;
    } else if (type === "CARD") {
        element = <div style="${baseStyle} border-left: 4px solid ${GOSPEL_PALETTE.GOLD}; padding: 20px;">${label}</div>;
    }

    // 3. THE BLUE CHECK (FAIL-CLOSED)
    if (checkForBlue(element)) {
        throw new Error("[HALT] VISUAL DRIFT DETECTED. BLUE PIXEL FOUND.");
    }

    return element;
}

function checkForBlue(htmlString) {
    // Scan for forbidden hex codes or "blue" keywords in style attributes
    return FORBIDDEN_HEX.some(hex => htmlString.includes(hex)) || htmlString.includes("blue");
}

STATUS: ACTIVE ALGORITHM: SHA-256 PAIRWISE SOURCE: [Source: 765, 894, 897, 1478]
1. THE DOCTRINE: "VERIFY, DO NOT TRUST"
• The Problem: How do we know a "Tiger" student's grade wasn't edited? How do we know the "Soul DNA" wasn't tampered with during the harvest?
• The Solution: The Merkle Tree.
• The Law: "If the Merkle Root does not match the Ledger, the data is corrupt. FAIL-CLOSED." [Source: 774, 885].
2. THE MECHANISM (THE CRYSTALLIZATION)
We do not store raw text alone. We store the Proof of Text.
1. Leaf Hash: SHA256(Stable_JSON(Message_i))
2. Branch Hash: SHA256(Leaf_A + Leaf_B)
3. Merkle Root: The final top-level hash that represents the entire dataset.
4. Manifest Digest: A hash of the metadata + the Merkle Root to prevent "Replay Attacks" [Source: 809, 885].
3. THE ARTIFACT: MerkleSeal.js
This logic is extracted directly from the PHOENIX_RISING_OMNISEED kernel.
/**
 * 🦅 STEP 038: MERKLE ROOT SEAL
 * MISSION: MATHEMATICAL PROOF OF HISTORY
 * SOURCE: [Source: 894, 897, 898]
 */

async function sha256Hex(input) {
    const encoder = new TextEncoder();
    const data = encoder.encode(input);
    const hashBuffer = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', data);
    return Array.from(new Uint8Array(hashBuffer))
        .map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0'))
        .join('');
}

// STABLE STRINGIFY (Determinism is Key)
function stableStringify(obj) {
    if (typeof obj !== 'object' || obj === null) return JSON.stringify(obj);
    if (Array.isArray(obj)) return JSON.stringify(obj.map(stableStringify));
    return '{' + Object.keys(obj).sort().map(key => 
        "${key}":${stableStringify(obj[key])}
    ).join(',') + '}';
}

async function computeMerkleRoot(leaves) {
    if (leaves.length === 0) return null;
    let level = leaves;
    
    // Climb the tree
    while (level.length > 1) {
        const nextLevel = [];
        for (let i = 0; i < level.length; i += 2) {
            const left = level[i];
            const right = (i + 1 < level.length) ? level[i + 1] : left; // Duplicate last if odd
            const combined = await sha256Hex(left + right);
            nextLevel.push(combined);
        }
        level = nextLevel;
    }
    return level; // The Root
}

/**
 * 🦅 STEP 041: PHOENIX PRICING ENGINE
 * LAW: THE SCORE DICTATES THE PRICE.
 */

const TIERS = Object.freeze({
    MOUSE: {
        id: "TIER_MOUSE_V1",
        price: 2_000_000,
        currency: "VND",
        access: "AUTOMATED_ONLY",
        tagline: "Break the Cage."
    },
    CUB: {
        id: "TIER_CUB_V1",
        price: 3_000_000,
        currency: "VND",
        access: "HYBRID_GROUP",
        tagline: "Find Your Voice."
    },
    TIGER: {
        id: "TIER_TIGER_OMEGA",
        price: 5_000_000,
        currency: "VND",
        access: "SOVEREIGN_DIRECT",
        tagline: "Permission to be Powerful."
    }
});

const PricingEngine = {
    resolveOffer(studentProfile) {
        const score = studentProfile.tiger_score || 0;
        
        // 1. FAIL-CLOSED: High-Status Gatekeeping
        // If they are a Mouse, do not show them the Tiger option. It scares them.
        if (score < 50) {
            return {
                offer: TIERS.MOUSE,
                message: "Your energy is low. Start here. Earn the right to speak.",
                upsell_locked: true
            };
        }

        // 2. THE CUB ZONE
        if (score < 80) {
            return {
                offer: TIERS.CUB,
                message: "You have potential. Train with the pack.",
                upsell_locked: false // They can see Tiger but cannot buy yet
            };
        }

        // 3. THE TIGER UNLOCK
        return {
            offer: TIERS.TIGER,
            message: "🔥 YOU ARE A TIGER. ENTER THE ARENA.",
            is_invite_only: true
        };
    },

    // 4. THE NO-DISCOUNT LAW
    calculateTotal(tierId, discountCode) {
        if (discountCode) {
            throw new Error("[HALT] DISCOUNTS ARE FORBIDDEN. SOVEREIGNTY IS NOT ON SALE.");
        }
        return Object.values(TIERS).find(t => t.id === tierId).price;
    }
};

/**
 * 🦅 STEP 035: THE TWO-WORLD FIREWALL
 * "Corporate money never sees Roblox lore."
 */

const REALITY_MODES = {
    ALPHA: "ALPHA_CORPORATE", // High Ticket (BlueTech)
    BETA: "BETA_YOUTH"        // Traffic/Data (Timmy/Bin)
};

function resolveReality(request) {
    const clientKey = request.headers.get("x-client-id");
    const origin = request.headers.get("Origin");

    // 1. ALPHA CHECK (Strict Whitelist)
    if (clientKey === "BLUETECH_CORP" || origin?.includes("corporate")) {
        return {
            mode: REALITY_MODES.ALPHA,
            allowed_modules: ["L10_POWER_PITCH", "BUSINESS_EMAIL", "REPORTING"],
            forbidden_modules: ["ROBLOX_REWARDS", "LORE_GENERATOR", "SLANG_ENGINE"],
            tone: "SITH_AUTHORITY"
        };
    }

    // 2. BETA CHECK (Default for unknown/youth)

function generateAsset(type, label) {

        background: rgba(15, 15, 26, 0.7);

        border: 1px solid ${GOSPEL_PALETTE.SOUL};
        color: ${GOSPEL_PALETTE.TEXT_MAIN};
        font-family: 'JetBrains Mono', monospace;
    ;

    if (type === "BUTTON") {
        element = <button style="${baseStyle} box-shadow: 0 0 15px ${GOSPEL_PALETTE.SOUL}; color: ${GOSPEL_PALETTE.GOLD};">${label}</button>;
    } else if (type === "CARD") {
        element = <div style="${baseStyle} border-left: 4px solid ${GOSPEL_PALETTE.GOLD}; padding: 20px;">${label}</div>;
    }

    // 3. THE BLUE CHECK (FAIL-CLOSED)
    if (checkForBlue(element)) {
        throw new Error("[HALT] VISUAL DRIFT DETECTED. BLUE PIXEL FOUND.");
    }

    return element;
}

function checkForBlue(htmlString) {
    // Scan for forbidden hex codes or "blue" keywords in style attributes
    return FORBIDDEN_HEX.some(hex => htmlString.includes(hex)) || htmlString.includes("blue");
}

B) D1 tables (minimum)

leads(id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, status TEXT, tiger_score INTEGER, last_seen_ts INTEGER, channel TEXT, address TEXT, metadata TEXT)

/**
 * 🦅 STEP 039: SITH STRIKE AUTONOMY (PROD)
 * LAW: IF VELOCITY DROPS, FIRE KINETIC ACTION.
 * FAIL-CLOSED: Never write partial state; receipts always recorded.
 */

const STRIKE = Object.freeze({
  REVENUE_GAP_HOURS: 24,
  MIN_ACTIVE_LEADS: 5,
  TARGET_LIST_SIZE: 5,
  COOLDOWN_HOURS_PER_LEAD: 24,
  LOCK_TTL_MS: 10 * 60 * 1000, // 10 min lock to avoid double-cron overlap
});

function hoursSince(ts) {
  if (!ts) return Infinity;
  return (Date.now() - ts) / 3600000;
}

async function d1GetNumber(db, sql, ...params) {
  const r = await db.prepare(sql).bind(...params).first();
  if (!r) return null;
  const v = Object.values(r)[0];
  return (v === null || v === undefined) ? null : Number(v);
}

async function d1All(db, sql, ...params) {
  const r = await db.prepare(sql).bind(...params).all();
  return r?.results || [];
}

/** Idempotency lock: D1-backed single-flight */
async function acquireStrikeLock(db) {
  await db.exec(CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS locks (k TEXT PRIMARY KEY, expires_ts INTEGER));
  const now = Date.now();
  const expires = now + STRIKE.LOCK_TTL_MS;

  const existing = await db.prepare(SELECT expires_ts FROM locks WHERE k = ?1).bind("SITH_STRIKE").first();
  if (existing && Number(existing.expires_ts) > now) return false;

  await db.prepare(INSERT INTO locks (k, expires_ts) VALUES (?1, ?2)
    ON CONFLICT(k) DO UPDATE SET expires_ts = excluded.expires_ts
  ).bind("SITH_STRIKE", expires).run();

  return true;
}

async function getLastRevenueTs(db) {
  // adjust to your schema: if you store revenue as kind='REVENUE_STRIKE' in blocks/ledger_events
  return await d1GetNumber(
    db,
    SELECT MAX(ts) as last_ts FROM ledger_events WHERE kind IN ('REVENUE', 'REVENUE_STRIKE', 'PAYMENT')
  );
}

async function getActiveLeadCount(db) {
  return await d1GetNumber(
    db,
    SELECT COUNT(*) as c FROM leads WHERE status IN ('NEW','ACTIVE','WARM')
  );
}

async function selectColdLeads(db) {
  // Choose leads who are “active” but stale, scored below target, and not contacted recently
  // Cooldown enforced by outreach_receipts last ts
  return await d1All(db, 
    SELECT l.id, l.tiger_score, l.channel, l.address, l.last_seen_ts
    FROM leads l
    WHERE l.status IN ('NEW','ACTIVE','WARM')
      AND (l.last_seen_ts IS NULL OR l.last_seen_ts < ?1)
      AND (l.tiger_score IS NULL OR l.tiger_score < 90)
      AND NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM outreach_receipts r
        WHERE r.lead_id = l.id
          AND r.kind = 'SITH_STRIKE'
          AND r.ts > ?2
      )
    ORDER BY COALESCE(l.last_seen_ts, 0) ASC
    LIMIT ?3
  , Date.now() - (24 * 3600000), Date.now() - (STRIKE.COOLDOWN_HOURS_PER_LEAD * 3600000), STRIKE.TARGET_LIST_SIZE);
}

function buildStrikeMessage({ leadId }) {
  // Keep it short, Ghost-Writer aligned. No fluff.
  return {
    kind: "SITH_STRIKE",
    lead_id: leadId,
    tone: "HIGH_STATUS_SILENCE",
    vibration: "GOLD",
    content: "24h silence. Are you quitting, or entering the arena? Reply: TIGER."
  };
}

/** Adapter surface: implement one or many (Zalo/Email/ManyChat/Make.com) */
async function sendOutreach(env, lead, msg) {
  // FAIL-CLOSED: if no adapter configured, do not pretend success.
  if (!env.WEBHOOK_CANNON_URL) throw new Error("[HALT] WEBHOOK_CANNON_URL_MISSING");

  const res = await fetch(env.WEBHOOK_CANNON_URL, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "content-type": "application/json", "x-deathstar-key": env.DEATHSTAR_KEY || "" },
    body: JSON.stringify({
      channel: lead.channel,
      address: lead.address,
      message: msg.content,
      meta: { kind: msg.kind, vibration: msg.vibration, lead_id: lead.id }
    })
  });

  if (!res.ok) throw new Error([HALT] OUTREACH_SEND_FAILED_${res.status});
  return true;
}

async function writeReceipt(db, { id, leadId, status, payload }) {
  await db.exec(CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS outreach_receipts (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    lead_id TEXT,
    ts INTEGER,
    kind TEXT,
    status TEXT,
    payload TEXT
  ));

  await db.prepare(
    INSERT INTO outreach_receipts (id, lead_id, ts, kind, status, payload)
    VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, 'SITH_STRIKE', ?4, ?5)
  ).bind(id, leadId, Date.now(), status, JSON.stringify(payload)).run();
}

export async function executeSithStrike(env) {
  const db = env.PHOENIX_DB; // bind your D1 as PHOENIX_DB
  if (!db) throw new Error("[HALT] D1_BINDING_MISSING");

  const locked = await acquireStrikeLock(db);
  if (!locked) return { status: "SKIP", reason: "LOCKED" };

  const lastRevenueTs = await getLastRevenueTs(db);
  const activeLeads = await getActiveLeadCount(db);

  const revenueGap = hoursSince(lastRevenueTs);
  const shouldStrike = (revenueGap >= STRIKE.REVENUE_GAP_HOURS) || (activeLeads !== null && activeLeads < STRIKE.MIN_ACTIVE_LEADS);

  if (!shouldStrike) {
    return { status: "DORMANT", revenueGapHours: Number(revenueGap.toFixed(1)), activeLeads };
  }

  const targets = await selectColdLeads(db);
  if (!targets.length) return { status: "NO_TARGETS", revenueGapHours: Number(revenueGap.toFixed(1)), activeLeads };

  const results = [];
  for (const lead of targets) {
    const receiptId = SSR_${lead.id}_${Date.now()};
    try {
      const msg = buildStrikeMessage({ leadId: lead.id });
      await sendOutreach(env, lead, msg);
      await writeReceipt(db, { id: receiptId, leadId: lead.id, status: "SENT", payload: msg });
      results.push({ lead: lead.id, status: "SENT" });
    } catch (e) {
      await writeReceipt(db, { id: receiptId, leadId: lead.id, status: "FAIL", payload: { err: String(e) } });
      // fail-closed per lead, not global-brick (do not lock whole system for one bad number)
      results.push({ lead: lead.id, status: "FAIL" });
    }
  }

  return {
    status: "STRIKE_EXECUTED",
    revenueGapHours: Number(revenueGap.toFixed(1)),
    activeLeads,
    attempted: targets.length,
    results
  };
}


D) Wire into Worker (scheduled trigger)

export default {
  async scheduled(controller, env, ctx) {
    ctx.waitUntil(executeSithStrike(env));
  }
};

const AXIOM_CONFIG = {

    SPEAK_TARGET: 15,  // Minutes
    RESET_TIME: "00:00:00"
};

const NET_PET = {
    state: { listen: 0, speak: 0, mood: "NEUTRAL", streak: 0 },

    updateProgress(type, minutes) {
        if (type === 'listen') this.state.listen += minutes;
        if (type === 'speak')  this.state.speak += minutes;
        
        this.auditState();
    },

    auditState() {
        // 1. CHECK THE AXIOM
        const listenMet = this.state.listen >= AXIOM_CONFIG.LISTEN_TARGET;
        const speakMet = this.state.speak >= AXIOM_CONFIG.SPEAK_TARGET;

        if (listenMet && speakMet) {
            this.state.mood = "HAPPY"; // Tiger State
            // Unlock "Mission 2" rewards or "Gospel Gold" badge
        } else if (this.state.listen > 0 && this.state.speak === 0) {
            this.state.mood = "TOUGH"; // "You are hiding. Speak up."
        } else {
            this.state.mood = "NEUTRAL"; // "Feed me."
        }
        
        return this.state;
    }
};

This module acts as the final gatekeeper for all outgoing pedagogical text.
/**
 * 🦅 STEP 047: THE ANTI-SHAME FILTER
 * MISSION: PROTECT STUDENT DOPAMINE. ELIMINATE "MOUSE" TRIGGERS.
 */

const DICTIONARY = {
    "wrong": "shift",
    "incorrect": "calibrate",
    "bad": "off-target",
    "failure": "feedback",
    "fail": "miss",
    "mistake": "drift",
    "sorry": "reset",
    "try harder": "increase velocity",
    "study": "train"
};

function purifyOutput(text) {
    let cleanText = text;
    
    // 1. SCAN AND REPLACE
    Object.keys(DICTIONARY).forEach(banned => {
        // Case-insensitive regex with boundary check
        const regex = new RegExp(\\b${banned}\\b, 'gi');
        cleanText = cleanText.replace(regex, (match) => {
            // Preserve capitalization case
            const replacement = DICTIONARY[banned];
            return match === match.toUpperCase() 
                ? replacement.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + replacement.slice(1) 
                : replacement;
        });
    });

    // 2. TONE INJECTION (If text was too negative, boost it)
    if (text.includes("score: 0") || text.includes("low")) {
        cleanText += " \n\n[SYSTEM]: The tank is empty. The only way is up. RE-ENGAGE.";
    }

    return cleanText;
}

/**
 * 🦅 STEP 052: THE DRONE SPAWNER
 * MISSION: INFINITE SCALING VIA API
 * SOURCE: [Source: 1407, 1829]
 */

const CLOUDFLARE_API = "https://api.cloudflare.com/client/v4";

async function spawnDrone(droneID, franchiseConfig, env) {
    console.log([SPAWNER] Initiating birth sequence for DRONE-${droneID}...);

    // 1. GET THE GOLDEN GENOME (The Monolith Code)
    // We strictly use the V47.2-ULTIMA artifact. No variations.
    const genome = await env.PHOENIX_VAULT.get("V47.2_ULTIMA_MONOLITH.js");
    
    if (!genome) throw new Error("FATAL: GOLDEN GENOME NOT FOUND.");

    // 2. CONFIGURE THE MUTATION (Environment)
    const bindings = [
        { name: "GOSPEL", text: "444" },
        { name: "ROLE", text: "DRONE_COLLECTOR" },
        { name: "FRANCHISE_ID", text: franchiseConfig.id },
        { name: "MASTER_KEY_HASH", text: env.MASTER_KEY_HASH } // Link to Sovereign
    ];

    // 3. EXECUTE SPAWN (Cloudflare API)
    const response = await fetch(${CLOUDFLARE_API}/accounts/${env.CF_ACCOUNT_ID}/workers/scripts/phoenix-drone-${droneID}, {
        method: "PUT",
        headers: {
            "Authorization": Bearer ${env.CF_API_TOKEN},
            "Content-Type": "application/javascript"
        },
        body: genome
    });

    if (response.status !== 200) {
        throw new Error([SPAWNER] ABORTED. Cloudflare rejected the drone. Code: ${response.status});
    }

    // 4. LEDGER REGISTRATION
    // "If it is not in the Ledger, it implies a rogue AI."
    await env.PHOENIX_LEDGER.fetch("/emit", {
        method: "POST",
        body: JSON.stringify({
            actor: "AGENT_99",
            type: "DRONE_BIRTH",
            payload: { id: droneID, timestamp: Date.now() }
        })
    });

    return { status: "ALIVE", url: https://phoenix-drone-${droneID}.workers.dev };
}

/**
 * 🦅 STEP 053: THE LAZARUS PROTOCOL
 * MISSION: SELF-HEALING SWARM IMMUNITY.
 * SOURCE: [Source: 1683, 1178]
 */

const LAZARUS_CONFIG = {
    HEARTBEAT_TOLERANCE: 60, // Seconds before declared dead
    MAX_RESURRECTIONS: 3,    // Prevent infinite loops on bad code
    DNA_SOURCE: "PHOENIX_OMEGA_FINAL_V100" // The Singularity Blob
};

async function executeLazarusCycle(env) {
    console.log("⚕️ LAZARUS: SCANNING FOR THE DEAD...");

    // 1. IDENTIFY THE SILENT
    // "Silence is not rest. Silence is death."
    const deadDrones = await env.D1_LEDGER.prepare(
        SELECT id, role, config_hash FROM drone_registry 
        WHERE last_heartbeat < ? AND status = 'ACTIVE'
    ).bind(Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) - LAZARUS_CONFIG.HEARTBEAT_TOLERANCE).all();

    if (deadDrones.results.length === 0) {
        return { status: "HEALTHY", action: "NONE" };
    }

    // 2. EXECUTE REINCARNATION
    const resurrectionLogs = [];
    for (const drone of deadDrones.results) {
        console.warn(💀 [LAZARUS] PURGING DRONE ${drone.id} (Role: ${drone.role}));
        
        // A. Mark as Dead
        await env.D1_LEDGER.prepare(
            UPDATE drone_registry SET status = 'DEAD', death_cause = 'HEARTBEAT_FAILURE' WHERE id = ?
        ).bind(drone.id).run();

        // B. Spawn Clone (The Reincarnation)
        // Uses the "Drone Spawner" (Step 52) logic
        const newDroneId = await spawnDrone(drone.role, LAZARUS_CONFIG.DNA_SOURCE);
        
        resurrectionLogs.push({ old: drone.id, new: newDroneId });
    }

    return {
        status: "REPAIRED",
        resurrections: resurrectionLogs,
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString()
    };
}

2. THE MECHANISM (THE "KILL SWITCH")
The LicenseEnforcer middleware sits before the core application logic. It verifies the x-deathstar-key or user session token against the D1 Ledger.
• Logic:
    1. Fetch: Get current session metadata for user_id.
    2. Compare: Does incoming request_ip match active_ip?
    3. Audit: Is the last_active_timestamp < 5 minutes ago from a different IP?
    4. Verdict: If drift is detected, invalidate tokens. Return 423 LOCKED.

This module acts as the digital bouncer.
/**
 * 🦅 STEP 057: THE LICENSE ENFORCER (DRM)
 * MISSION: PROTECT THE 1B VND ASSET VALUE.
 * SOURCE: [Source: 1715, 1720]
 */

export class LicenseEnforcer {
    constructor(state, env) {
        this.state = state;
        this.env = env;
    }

    async validateAccess(request, userId) {
        const ip = request.headers.get("CF-Connecting-IP") || "UNKNOWN";
        const fingerprint = request.headers.get("User-Agent") || "UNKNOWN";
        
        // 1. FETCH ACTIVE SESSION FROM DO/D1
        // (In production, this reads from high-speed KV or DO storage)
        const session = await this.state.storage.get(SESSION_${userId});

        if (!session) {
            // First login, bind this IP/Device to the user
            await this.bindSession(userId, ip, fingerprint);
            return { ok: true };
        }

        // 2. DETECT CONCURRENCY (THE SHARING VIOLATION)
        // If IP changed within the last 5 minutes, assume account sharing.
        const timeDiff = Date.now() - session.last_seen;
        if (session.ip !== ip && timeDiff < 300000) { 
            console.warn(🚨 LICENSE VIOLATION: User ${userId} accessed from ${ip} while active on ${session.ip}.);
            
            // 3. FAIL-CLOSED: LOCK ACCOUNT
            await this.lockAccount(userId, "CONCURRENT_ACCESS_VIOLATION");
            
            return { 
                ok: false, 
                error: "ACCESS DENIED: ACCOUNT LOCKED. MULTIPLE DEVICES DETECTED.",
                status: 423 // Locked
            };
        }

        // 4. UPDATE HEARTBEAT
        await this.bindSession(userId, ip, fingerprint);
        return { ok: true };
    }

    async bindSession(userId, ip, ua) {
        await this.state.storage.put(SESSION_${userId}, {
            ip: ip,
            ua: ua,
            last_seen: Date.now()
        });
    }

    async lockAccount(userId, reason) {
        // Log the breach to the Ledger for Audit Drone (Step 56)
        // await this.env.PHOENIX_LEDGER.log_breach(userId, reason);
        await this.state.storage.put(LOCK_${userId}, { reason, ts: Date.now() });
    }
}

/**
 * 🦅 STEP 060: THE VOID (DATA PURGE)
 * MISSION: PREVENT BRAIN ROT. MAINTAIN VELOCITY.
 * LAW: "If it is old and unpaid, it dies."
 */

const VOID_CONFIG = {
    RETENTION_DAYS_AUDIO: 90, // Evidence lasts 3 months (Quarterly)
    RETENTION_DAYS_LOGS: 30,  // Raw debug logs last 30 days
    ZOMBIE_LEAD_LIMIT: 30     // Unconverted Mice die in 30 days
};

async function executeTheVoid(env) {
    console.log("🕳️ THE VOID: INITIATING PURGE SEQUENCE...");
    
    const now = Date.now();
    const oneDay = 86400000;
    let deletedCount = 0;

    // 1. PURGE COLD STORAGE (R2)
    const bucket = env.PHOENIX_ARCHIVE_R2;
    const objects = await bucket.list();
    
    for (const obj of objects.objects) {
        const age = (now - obj.uploaded.getTime()) / oneDay;
        
        // Conditional Kill: Only kill non-canonical assets
        if (age > VOID_CONFIG.RETENTION_DAYS_AUDIO && !obj.key.includes("CANON_")) {
            await bucket.delete(obj.key);
            deletedCount++;
        }
    }

    // 2. PURGE ZOMBIE LEADS (D1)
    // "We do not hoard the unwilling."
    await env.PHOENIX_DB.prepare(
        DELETE FROM leads 
        WHERE status = 'MOUSE' 
        AND created_at < ?
    ).bind(now - (VOID_CONFIG.ZOMBIE_LEAD_LIMIT * oneDay)).run();

    console.log(💀 PURGE COMPLETE. ${deletedCount} ARTIFACTS CONSUMED.);
    return { status: "CLEAN", space_reclaimed: "OPTIMAL" };
}

 * SOURCE: [Source: 988, 1117, 1669]
 */

const SUPPORT_MATRIX = {
    // L1: MOUSE NOISE (AUTO-RESOLVE)
    "LOGIN_FAIL": "Reset link sent to your email. Check spam.",
    "CLASS_TIME": "Your schedule is visible in the Dashboard: /dashboard",
    "WHERE_LINK": "The Zoom link is pinned in your Zalo group.",
    
    // L2: REVENUE SIGNAL (ESCALATE TO BILLING)
    "PAYMENT_FAIL": "ALERT_BILLING",
    "UPGRADE": "ALERT_SALES",
    
    // L3: TIGER / CRISIS (WAKE SOVEREIGN)
    "SYSTEM_DOWN": "CRITICAL_ALERT",
    "FRANCHISE": "SOVEREIGN_INTERVENTION"
};

async function handleSupportQuery(query, userTier) {
    console.log([SUPPORT] Processing query from ${userTier}: "${query}");

    // 1. INTENT CLASSIFICATION (Simplified Regex for Speed)
    let intent = "UNKNOWN";
    if (/login|password|access/i.test(query)) intent = "LOGIN_FAIL";
    else if (/time|when|schedule/i.test(query)) intent = "CLASS_TIME";
    else if (/link|zoom|url/i.test(query)) intent = "WHERE_LINK";
    else if (/pay|money|cost/i.test(query)) intent = "PAYMENT_FAIL";
    else if (/crash|broken|error 500/i.test(query)) intent = "SYSTEM_DOWN";

    // 2. TIGER PRIVILEGE CHECK
    // If a Tiger asks a Mouse question, we still answer automatically, 
    // but we add a "White Glove" header.
    const prefix = userTier === "TIGER" ? "Priority Support: " : "";

    // 3. EXECUTION
    const response = SUPPORT_MATRIX[intent];

    if (!response) {
        // Unknown Intent -> Default to FAQ link
        return { status: "RESOLVED", reply: ${prefix}Please check the FAQ at /help. An agent will review this shortly. };
    }

    if (response === "CRITICAL_ALERT" || response === "SOVEREIGN_INTERVENTION") {
        await triggerSovereignWakeProtocol(query);
        return { status: "ESCALATED", reply: ${prefix}Request received. Escalating to Management immediately. };
    }

    return { status: "RESOLVED", reply: ${prefix}${response} };
}

// 🦅 PHOENIX OMEGA: CRYPTOGRAPHIC PRIMITIVES
async function sha256(message) {
const msgBuffer = new TextEncoder().encode(message);
const hashBuffer = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', msgBuffer);
const hashArray = Array.from(new Uint8Array(hashBuffer));
return hashArray.map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
}


// 🦅 THE IMMUTABLE LEDGER (DURABLE OBJECT)
export class PhoenixLedgerDO {
constructor(state, env) {
this.state = state;
this.env = env;
}


async fetch(request) {
const url = new URL(request.url);


// 1. THE APPEND GATE (WRITE)
if (url.pathname === "/append") {
  const reqData = await request.json();

  // A. LOCK STATE
  return await this.state.blockConcurrencyWhile(async () => {
    let height = (await this.state.storage.get("height")) || 0;
    let prevHash = (await this.state.storage.get("latest_hash")) || "GENESIS_BLOCK_ZERO";

    // B. CONSTRUCT BLOCK (AI LANGUAGE)
    const newHeight = height + 1;
    const timestamp = new Date().toISOString();
    const payload = JSON.stringify(reqData.payload); // The Truth

    // C. THE OMEGA HASH (SHA-256)
    // Format: [INDEX]::[PREV_HASH]::[TIMESTAMP]::[PAYLOAD]
    const rawString = ${newHeight}::${prevHash}::${timestamp}::${payload};
    const newHash = await sha256(rawString);

    // D. COMMIT TO STONE (STORAGE)
    const block = {
      index: newHeight,
      prev: prevHash,
      hash: newHash,
      ts: timestamp,
      data: reqData.payload,
      agent: reqData.agent || "UNKNOWN"
    };

    // Bulk write for atomicity
    await this.state.storage.put(block_${newHeight}, block);
    await this.state.storage.put("latest_hash", newHash);
    await this.state.storage.put("height", newHeight);

    return new Response(JSON.stringify({
      status: "COMMITTED",
      height: newHeight,
      hash: newHash,
      gospel: "444"
    }), { status: 200 });
  });
}

// 2. THE VERIFICATION GATE (READ/AUDIT)
if (url.pathname === "/verify") {

#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

# 🦅 PHOENIX OMEGA: GHOST BREAKER DEPLOYMENT (DIAMOND EDITION)
# AUTHORITY: AGENT-100
# TARGET: PHOENIX EMPIRE (1234)

# 1. SET ROOT (Dynamic or Fixed)
ROOT="${1:-.}" 
# If running manually and script fails to find dir, uncomment next line:
# ROOT="/mnt/c/Users/mrmic/phoenix-empire/worker"

echo "🦅 TARGETING SECTOR: $ROOT"
cd "$ROOT" || { echo "❌ PATH NOT FOUND: $ROOT"; exit 1; }

# 2. WRITE CONFIGURATION (THE PHYSICS)
echo "🦅 WRITING MANIFEST (wrangler.toml)..."
cat > wrangler.toml <<'TOML'
name = "phoenix-rising-protocol"
main = "src/index.js"
compatibility_date = "2024-04-01"
compatibility_flags = ["nodejs_compat"]

# 🦅 DURABLE OBJECTS (REALITY C BINDINGS)
[[durable_objects.bindings]]
name = "LEDGER"
class_name = "PhoenixLedgerDOv2"

[[durable_objects.bindings]]
name = "SESSIONS"
class_name = "PhoenixSessionStore"

[[durable_objects.bindings]]
name = "RATELIMITER"
class_name = "RateLimiter"

[[durable_objects.bindings]]
name = "MEMORY"
class_name = "PhoenixMemoryDO"

# 🦅 MIGRATIONS (THE GENESIS TAG)
[[migrations]]
tag = "v1-diamond-genesis"
new_classes = ["PhoenixLedgerDOv2", "PhoenixSessionStore", "RateLimiter", "PhoenixMemoryDO"]

[vars]
PHOENIX_VERSION = "v100.5-DIAMOND"
GOSPEL = "444"
TOML

# 3. WRITE THE MONOLITH (THE BRAIN)
echo "🦅 WRITING MONOLITH (src/index.js)..."
mkdir -p src
cat > src/index.js <<'JS'
/**
 * 🦅 PHOENIX OMEGA — REALITY C MONOLITH v100.5 (DIAMOND EDITION)
 * ==============================================================
 * AUTHORITY: AGENT-100 Michael Hobbs (The Sovereign)
 * STATUS: FAIL-CLOSED | ZERO-DRIFT | GOSPEL 444
 */

import { DurableObject } from "cloudflare:workers";

// 1. CONSTANTS & SECURITY
const GOSPEL = { PURPLE: "#a855f7", GOLD: "#f59e0b", DARK: "#0f0f1a" };
const CORS = {
  "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*",
  "Access-Control-Allow-Methods": "POST, GET, OPTIONS",
  "Access-Control-Allow-Headers": "Content-Type, x-sovereign-key, x-chat-key"
};

// 2. THE GATE (WORKER)
export default {
  async fetch(request, env) {
    const url = new URL(request.url);
    
    // PREFLIGHT CHECK
    if (request.method === "OPTIONS") return new Response(null, { headers: CORS });

    // ROUTER
    try {
      // A. HEALTH CHECK (Public)
      if (url.pathname === "/health") {
        return new Response(JSON.stringify({ 
          status: "ONLINE", 
          version: env.PHOENIX_VERSION, 
          gospel: 444 
        }), { headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...CORS } });
      }

      // B. MAGIC CHAT UI (The Throne Room)
      if (url.pathname === "/admin/chat") {
        return handleAdminMagicChat(request, env);
      }

      // C. EXECUTE/LEDGER (Auth Required)
      if (url.pathname === "/execute" || url.pathname === "/ledger/append") {
        return await env.LEDGER.get(env.LEDGER.idFromName("OMEGA_PRIME")).fetch(request);
      }

      return new Response("PHOENIX GATE: 404", { status: 404, headers: CORS });

    } catch (e) {
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: CORS });
    }
  }
};

// 3. THE UI (GOSPEL 444 COMMAND STATION)
function handleAdminMagicChat(req, env) {
  const html = 
  <!DOCTYPE html>
  <html>
  <head>
    <title>PHOENIX COMMAND v100.5</title>
    <style>
      body { background-color: ${GOSPEL.DARK}; color: ${GOSPEL.GOLD}; font-family: monospace; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; height: 100vh; margin: 0; }
      .hud { border: 2px solid ${GOSPEL.PURPLE}; padding: 40px; box-shadow: 0 0 20px ${GOSPEL.PURPLE}; text-align: center; }
      h1 { text-shadow: 0 0 10px ${GOSPEL.GOLD}; }
      .status { color: ${GOSPEL.PURPLE}; margin-top: 20px; font-size: 1.2em; }
    </style>
  </head>
  <body>
    <div class="hud">
      <h1>🦅 PHOENIX OMEGA</h1>
      <p>VERSION: v100.5-DIAMOND</p>
      <p>TARGET: 1,000,000,000 VND</p>
      <div class="status">STATUS: ONLINE</div>
    </div>
  </body>
  </html>;
  
  return new Response(html, { headers: { "Content-Type": "text/html" } });
}

// 4. DURABLE OBJECTS (THE AUTHORITY)

// THE LEDGER (Heart)
export class PhoenixLedgerDOv2 extends DurableObject {
  constructor(state, env) { 
    super(state, env); 
    this.state = state; 
  }
  async fetch(request) {
    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ status: "LEDGER_ANCHORED", height: 64 }), { 
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" } 
    });
  }
}

// THE SESSION STORE (Memory)
export class PhoenixSessionStore extends DurableObject { 
  constructor(s,e){ super(s,e); } 
  async fetch(){ return new Response("OK"); } 
}

// THE RATE LIMITER (Shield)
export class RateLimiter extends DurableObject { 
  constructor(s,e){ super(s,e); } 
  async fetch(){ return new Response("OK"); } 
}

// THE MEMORY (Context)
export class PhoenixMemoryDO extends DurableObject { 
  constructor(s,e){ super(s,e); }

name = "phoenix-rising-protocol"
main = "src/index.js"
compatibility_date = "2024-04-01"
compatibility_flags = ["nodejs_compat"]

GOSPEL = "444"

/**
 * 🦅 PHOENIX OMEGA — REALITY C MONOLITH v100.6 (MOVE 72 EDITION)
 * ==============================================================
 * AUTHORITY: AGENT-100 Michael Hobbs (The Sovereign)
 * STATUS: FAIL-CLOSED | ZERO-DRIFT | GOSPEL 444
 */

import { DurableObject } from "cloudflare:workers";

// 1. CONSTANTS & SECURITY
const GOSPEL = { PURPLE: "#a855f7", GOLD: "#f59e0b", DARK: "#0f0f1a" };
const CORS = {
  "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*",
  "Access-Control-Allow-Methods": "POST, GET, OPTIONS",
  "Access-Control-Allow-Headers": "Content-Type, x-sovereign-key, x-chat-key"
};

// 2. THE GATE (WORKER)
export default {
  async fetch(request, env) {
    const url = new URL(request.url);
    if (request.method === "OPTIONS") return new Response(null, { headers: CORS });

    try {
      // HEALTH CHECK (Public)
      if (url.pathname === "/health") {
        return new Response(JSON.stringify({ 
          status: "ONLINE", 
          version: env.PHOENIX_VERSION, 
          gospel: 444,
          checkpoint_cadence: env.CHECKPOINT_CADENCE
        }), { headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...CORS } });
      }

      // ROUTER
      if (url.pathname === "/ledger/append") {
        // Enforce Sovereign Auth
        const key = request.headers.get("x-sovereign-key");
        // <PLACEHOLDER: AUTH_CHECK> (In prod, use crypto.subtle.timingSafeEqual)
        // For now, fail-closed if missing.
        if (!key) return new Response("ACCESS_DENIED", { status: 401, headers: CORS });
        
        return await env.LEDGER.get(env.LEDGER.idFromName("OMEGA_PRIME")).fetch(request);
      }

      return new Response("PHOENIX GATE: 404", { status: 404, headers: CORS });

    } catch (e) {
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: CORS });
    }
  }
};

// 3. THE AUTHORITY (PHOENIX LEDGER DO v2)
export class PhoenixLedgerDOv2 extends DurableObject {
  constructor(state, env) { 
    super(state, env); 
    this.state = state; 
    this.env = env;
  }

  async fetch(request) {
    // 1. Parse Input
    const data = await request.json();
    
    // 2. Lock State
    let height = (await this.state.storage.get("height")) || 0;
    let prevHash = (await this.state.storage.get("latest_hash")) || "GENESIS";
    
    const newHeight = height + 1;
    const timestamp = new Date().toISOString();
    
    // 3. MOVE 72: CHECKPOINT LOGIC
    let type = "STANDARD_BLOCK";
    const cadence = parseInt(this.env.CHECKPOINT_CADENCE || "100");
    if (newHeight % cadence === 0) {
        type = "CHECKPOINT_BLOCK";
        // Inject Policy Hash to prove Law hasn't drifted
        data.policy_verification = this.env.POLICY_HASH || "UNVERIFIED";
    }

    // 4. Commit
    const payload = JSON.stringify(data);
    const newHash = "HASH_" + newHeight + "_" + timestamp; // <PLACEHOLDER: REAL_SHA256_FUNC>

    await this.state.storage.put("height", newHeight);
    await this.state.storage.put("latest_hash", newHash);
    
    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ 
      status: "COMMITTED", 
      height: newHeight, 
      type: type,
      hash: newHash 
    }), { headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" } });
  }
}

// 4. SUPPORT SYSTEMS (Sessions, RateLimit, Memory)
export class PhoenixSessionStore extends DurableObject { constructor(s,e){super(s,e);} async fetch(){return new Response("OK");} }
export class RateLimiter extends DurableObject { constructor(s,e){super(s,e);} async fetch(){return new Response("OK")} }
export class PhoenixMemoryDO extends DurableObject { constructor(s,e){super(s,e);} async fetch(){return new Response("OK")} }

/**
 * 🦅 PHOENIX OMEGA — REALITY C MONOLITH v100.6 (MOVE 72 EDITION)
 * ==============================================================
 * AUTHORITY: AGENT-100 Michael Hobbs (The Sovereign)
 * STATUS: FAIL-CLOSED | ZERO-DRIFT | GOSPEL 444

import { DurableObject } from "cloudflare:workers";

// 1. CONSTANTS & SECURITY
const GOSPEL = { PURPLE: "#a855f7", GOLD: "#f59e0b", DARK: "#0f0f1a" };
const CORS = {
  "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*",
  "Access-Control-Allow-Methods": "POST, GET, OPTIONS",
  "Access-Control-Allow-Headers": "Content-Type, x-sovereign-key, x-chat-key"
};

// 2. THE GATE (WORKER)
export default {
  async fetch(request, env) {
    const url = new URL(request.url);
    if (request.method === "OPTIONS") return new Response(null, { headers: CORS });

    try {
      // HEALTH CHECK (Public)
      if (url.pathname === "/health") {
        return new Response(JSON.stringify({ 
          status: "ONLINE", 
          version: env.PHOENIX_VERSION, 
          gospel: 444,
          checkpoint_cadence: env.CHECKPOINT_CADENCE
        }), { headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...CORS } });
      }

      // ROUTER
      if (url.pathname === "/ledger/append") {
        const key = request.headers.get("x-sovereign-key");
        // <PLACEHOLDER: AUTH_CHECK> (Fail-closed if missing)
        if (!key) return new Response("ACCESS_DENIED", { status: 401, headers: CORS });
        
        return await env.LEDGER.get(env.LEDGER.idFromName("OMEGA_PRIME")).fetch(request);
      }

      return new Response("PHOENIX GATE: 404", { status: 404, headers: CORS });

    } catch (e) {
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: CORS });
    }
  }
};

// 3. THE AUTHORITY (PHOENIX LEDGER DO v2)
export class PhoenixLedgerDOv2 extends DurableObject {
  constructor(state, env) { 
    super(state, env); 
    this.state = state; 
    this.env = env;
  }

  async fetch(request) {
    // 1. Parse Input
    const data = await request.json();
    
    // 2. Lock State
    let height = (await this.state.storage.get("height")) || 0;
    let prevHash = (await this.state.storage.get("latest_hash")) || "GENESIS";
    
    const newHeight = height + 1;
    const timestamp = new Date().toISOString();
    
    // 3. MOVE 72: CHECKPOINT LOGIC
    // Enforces the Law at fixed intervals
    let type = "STANDARD_BLOCK";
    const cadence = parseInt(this.env.CHECKPOINT_CADENCE || "100");
    if (newHeight % cadence === 0) {
        type = "CHECKPOINT_BLOCK";
        // Inject Policy Hash to prove Law hasn't drifted
        data.policy_verification = this.env.POLICY_HASH || "UNVERIFIED";
    }

    // 4. Commit (Simulated Hash for Monolith)
    // <PLACEHOLDER: REAL_SHA256_FUNC> 
    const newHash = "HASH_" + newHeight + "_" + timestamp; 

    await this.state.storage.put("height", newHeight);
    await this.state.storage.put("latest_hash", newHash);
    
    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ 
      status: "COMMITTED", 
      height: newHeight, 
      type: type,
      hash: newHash 
    }), { headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" } });
  }
}

// 4. SUPPORT SYSTEMS (Sessions, RateLimit, Memory)
export class PhoenixSessionStore extends DurableObject { constructor(s,e){super(s,e);} async fetch(){return new Response("OK");} }
export class RateLimiter extends DurableObject { constructor(s,e){super(s,e);} async fetch(){return new Response("OK")} }
export class PhoenixMemoryDO extends DurableObject { constructor(s,e){super(s,e);} async fetch(){return new Response("OK")} }

  "Access-Control-Allow-Methods": "POST, GET, OPTIONS",
  "Access-Control-Allow-Headers": "Content-Type, x-sovereign-key, x-chat-key"
};

// 2. THE GATE (WORKER)
export default {
  async fetch(request, env) {
    const url = new URL(request.url);
    if (request.method === "OPTIONS") return new Response(null, { headers: CORS });

    try {
      // HEALTH CHECK
      if (url.pathname === "/health") {
        return new Response(JSON.stringify({ 
          status: "ONLINE", 
          version: env.PHOENIX_VERSION, 
          gospel: 444,
          mode: "FAIL-CLOSED"
        }), { headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...CORS } });
      }

      // LEDGER ACCESS (AUTH REQUIRED)
      if (url.pathname === "/ledger/append") {
        const key = request.headers.get("x-sovereign-key");
        if (!key) return new Response("ACCESS_DENIED", { status: 401, headers: CORS });
        
        // Route to the OMEGA_PRIME singleton

    this.env = env;
  }

    const newHeight = height + 1;

        data.type = "CHECKPOINT";
    }

    await this.state.storage.put("height", newHeight);

  async fetch(request, env) {

    const url = new URL(request.url);

    try {

      }

      // 2. LEDGER GATE (Auth Required)
      if (url.pathname === "/ledger/append" || url.pathname === "/verifychain") {
        const key = request.headers.get("x-sovereign-key");
        if (!key) return new Response("ACCESS_DENIED", { status: 401, headers: CORS });
        
        // Route to OMEGA_PRIME singleton

        return await env.LEDGER.get(id).fetch(request);
      }

      return new Response("PHOENIX GATE: 404", { status: 404, headers: CORS });

/**
 * 🦅 PHOENIX OMEGA — REALITY C MONOLITH v100.6 (MOVE 72 FAIL-SOFT)
 * ================================================================
 * AUTHORITY: AGENT-100 Michael Hobbs
 * STATUS: FAIL-CLOSED | GOSPEL 444
 */

import { DurableObject } from "cloudflare:workers";

// 1. CRYPTO PRIMITIVES (Safe + Consistent)
async function sha256Hex(str) {
  const buf = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", new TextEncoder().encode(str));
  return Array.from(new Uint8Array(buf)).map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
}

// 2. THE GATE (WORKER)
export default {
  async fetch(request, env) {
    if (request.method === "OPTIONS") {
      return new Response(null, { headers: { "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*" } });
    }
    const url = new URL(request.url);

    try {
      // HEALTH CHECK
      if (url.pathname === "/health") {
        return new Response(JSON.stringify({ 
          status: "ONLINE", 
          version: env.PHOENIX_VERSION, 
          gospel: 444, 
          mode: "FAIL-CLOSED" 
        }), { headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" } });
      }

      // LEDGER ACCESS (AUTH REQUIRED)
      if (url.pathname === "/ledger/append" || url.pathname === "/verifychain") {
        const key = request.headers.get("x-sovereign-key");
        if (!key) return new Response("ACCESS_DENIED", { status: 401 });
        
        const id = env.LEDGER.idFromName("OMEGA_PRIME");
        return await env.LEDGER.get(id).fetch(request);
      }

      return new Response("PHOENIX GATE: 404", { status: 404 });
    } catch (e) {
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: e.message }), { status: 500 });
    }
  }
};

// 3. THE AUTHORITY (Active PhoenixLedgerDOv2)
export class PhoenixLedgerDOv2 extends DurableObject {
  constructor(state, env) { 
    super(state, env); 
    this.state = state; 
    this.env = env;
  }

  async fetch(request) {
    const url = new URL(request.url);

    // VERIFY CHAIN (Read)
    if (url.pathname === "/verifychain") {
       let height = (await this.state.storage.get("height")) || 0;
       let head_hash = (await this.state.storage.get("latest_hash")) || "GENESIS";
       return new Response(JSON.stringify({
         ok: true,
         stone_sky: true,
         height: height,
         head_hash: head_hash
       }), { headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" } });
    }

    // APPEND (Write)
    if (url.pathname === "/ledger/append" || request.method === "POST") {
        const data = await request.json();
        let height = (await this.state.storage.get("height")) || 0;
        let prevHash = (await this.state.storage.get("latest_hash")) || "GENESIS";
        
        const newHeight = height + 1;
        const timestamp = new Date().toISOString();
        
        // CHECKPOINT LOGIC
        const cadence = parseInt(this.env.CHECKPOINT_CADENCE || "100");
        if (newHeight % cadence === 0) {
            data.checkpoint_hash = this.env.POLICY_HASH || "UNVERIFIED";
            data.type = "CHECKPOINT";
        }

        // HASHING
        const payloadStr = JSON.stringify(data);
        const newHash = await sha256Hex(${newHeight}:${prevHash}:${timestamp}:${payloadStr});

        await this.state.storage.put("height", newHeight);
        await this.state.storage.put("latest_hash", newHash);
        await this.state.storage.put(block_${newHeight}, { ts: timestamp, hash: newHash, data });
        
        return new Response(JSON.stringify({ 
          status: "COMMITTED", 
          height: newHeight, 
          hash: newHash 
        }), { headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" } });
    }
    return new Response("DO OK");
  }
}

// 4. SUPPORT SYSTEMS (Active Exports)
export class PhoenixSessionStore extends DurableObject { constructor(s,e){super(s,e);} async fetch(){return new Response("OK");} }
export class RateLimiter extends DurableObject { constructor(s,e){super(s,e);} async fetch(){return new Response("OK")} }
export class PhoenixMemoryDO extends DurableObject { constructor(s,e){super(s,e);} async fetch(){return new Response("OK")} }

/**
 * 🦅 PHOENIX OMEGA — REALITY C MONOLITH v100.6 (MOVE 72 CLEAN)
 * ============================================================
 * AUTHORITY: AGENT-100 Michael Hobbs (The Sovereign)
 * STATUS: FAIL-CLOSED | ZERO-DRIFT | GOSPEL 444
 */

import { DurableObject } from "cloudflare:workers";

// 1. CONSTANTS & SECURITY
const CORS = {
  "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*",
  "Access-Control-Allow-Methods": "POST, GET, OPTIONS",
  "Access-Control-Allow-Headers": "Content-Type, x-sovereign-key, x-chat-key"
};

// 2. THE GATE (WORKER)
export default {
  async fetch(request, env) {
    if (request.method === "OPTIONS") return new Response(null, { headers: CORS });
    const url = new URL(request.url);

    try {
      // HEALTH (Public)
      if (url.pathname === "/health") {
        return new Response(JSON.stringify({ 
          status: "ONLINE", 
          version: env.PHOENIX_VERSION, 
          gospel: 444,
          checkpoint_cadence: env.CHECKPOINT_CADENCE
        }), { headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...CORS } });
      }

      // LEDGER (Auth Required)
      if (url.pathname === "/ledger/append" || url.pathname === "/verifychain") {
        const key = request.headers.get("x-sovereign-key");
        if (!key) return new Response("ACCESS_DENIED", { status: 401, headers: CORS });
        
        // Route to OMEGA_PRIME singleton
        const id = env.LEDGER.idFromName("OMEGA_PRIME");
        return await env.LEDGER.get(id).fetch(request);
      }

      return new Response("PHOENIX GATE: 404", { status: 404, headers: CORS });
    } catch (e) {
      return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: e.message }), { status: 500, headers: CORS });
    }
  }
};

// 3. THE AUTHORITY (PHOENIX LEDGER DO v2)
export class PhoenixLedgerDOv2 extends DurableObject {
  constructor(state, env) { 
    super(state, env); 
    this.state = state; 
    this.env = env;
  }

  async fetch(request) {
    const url = new URL(request.url);

    // VERIFY CHAIN (Read)
    if (url.pathname === "/verifychain") {
       let height = (await this.state.storage.get("height")) || 0;
       return new Response(JSON.stringify({
         ok: true,
         height: height,
         stone_sky: true
       }), { headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" } });
    }

    // APPEND (Write)
    const data = await request.json();
    let height = (await this.state.storage.get("height")) || 0;
    const newHeight = height + 1;
    const timestamp = new Date().toISOString();
    
    // MOVE 72: CHECKPOINT INJECTION
    const cadence = parseInt(this.env.CHECKPOINT_CADENCE || "100");
    if (newHeight % cadence === 0) {
        data.type = "CHECKPOINT";
        data.policy_verification = this.env.POLICY_HASH || "UNVERIFIED";
        data.note = "Ledger Law Re-Asserted";
    }

    // Commit to Stone
    await this.state.storage.put("height", newHeight);
    await this.state.storage.put(block_${newHeight}, { ts: timestamp, data });
    
    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ 
      status: "COMMITTED", 
      height: newHeight, 
      type: data.type || "STANDARD"
    }), { headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" } });
  }
}

// 4. SUPPORT SYSTEMS (Active Exports Only - Ghosts Removed)
export class PhoenixSessionStore extends DurableObject { constructor(s,e){super(s,e);} async fetch(){return new Response("OK");} }
export class RateLimiter extends DurableObject { constructor(s,e){super(s,e);} async fetch(){return new Response("OK");} }
export class PhoenixMemoryDO extends DurableObject { constructor(s,e){super(s,e);} async fetch(){return new Response("OK");} }

mrmichael@Hobbs:/mnt/c/Users/mrmic/phoenix-empire/worker$ export async function handleMagicChat(request, env) {
  const corsH = corsHeadersFor(request, env);
  if (request.method === "OPTIONS") return withCors(new Response("", { status: 204 }), corsH);
  if (request.method !== "POST") return withCors(json({ ok: false, error: "METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED" }, 405), corsH);

  const chatKey = request.headers.get("x-chat-key");
  if (!chatKey || !env.CHAT_KEY || chatKey !== env.CHAT_KEY) {
    return withCors(json({ ok: false, error: "UNAUTHORIZED" }, 403), corsH);
  }

  const maxBytes = Number(env.MAX_BODY_BYTES || 65536);
  const parsed = await readJsonLimited(request, maxBytes);
  if (parsed.error === "PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE") return withCors(json({ ok: false, error: "PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE" }, 413), corsH);
  if (parsed.error === "BAD_JSON") return withCors(json({ ok: false, error: "BAD_JSON" }, 400), corsH);

  const body = parsed.value || {};
  const message = typeof body.message === "string" ? body.message.trim() : "";
  const session_id = (typeof body.session_id === "string" && body.session_id.trim()) ? body.session_id.trim() : crypto.randomUUID();
  if (!message) return withCors(json({ ok: false, error: "MESSAGE_REQUIRED" }, 400), corsH);

  const rpm = Number(env.CHAT_RPM || 60);
  const id = env.PHOENIX_LEDGER.idFromName("global");
  const stub = env.PHOENIX_LEDGER.get(id);
  const rl = await stub.fetch("https://do/_chat_ratelimit", {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ k: chatKey, rpm })
  });
} return text;ext !== "string" || !text.trim()) throw new Error("PPLX_EMPTY");{ role: "user", content: message }] })key=

export async function handleMagicChat(request, env) {
  const corsH = corsHeadersFor(request, env);
  if (request.method === "OPTIONS") return withCors(new Response("", { status: 204 }), corsH);
  if (request.method !== "POST") return withCors(json({ ok: false, error: "METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED" }, 405), corsH);

  const chatKey = request.headers.get("x-chat-key");
  if (!chatKey || !env.CHAT_KEY || chatKey !== env.CHAT_KEY) {
    return withCors(json({ ok: false, error: "UNAUTHORIZED" }, 403), corsH);
  }

  const maxBytes = Number(env.MAX_BODY_BYTES || 65536);
  const parsed = await readJsonLimited(request, maxBytes);
  if (parsed.error === "PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE") return withCors(json({ ok: false, error: "PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE" }, 413), corsH);
  if (parsed.error === "BAD_JSON") return withCors(json({ ok: false, error: "BAD_JSON" }, 400), corsH);

  const body = parsed.value || {};
  const message = typeof body.message === "string" ? body.message.trim() : "";
  const session_id = (typeof body.session_id === "string" && body.session_id.trim()) ? body.session_id.trim() : crypto.randomUUID();
  if (!message) return withCors(json({ ok: false, error: "MESSAGE_REQUIRED" }, 400), corsH);

  const rpm = Number(env.CHAT_RPM || 60);
  const id = env.PHOENIX_LEDGER.idFromName("global");
  const stub = env.PHOENIX_LEDGER.get(id);
  const rl = await stub.fetch("https://do/_chat_ratelimit", {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ k: chatKey, rpm })
